| D13 | Wellbeing radar (opt-in, privacy-first) | **Done** | **Migration 0125** (`wellbeing_settings`: opted in, counsellor consent, time zone, last nudge; `wellbeing_checkins`: one per local day: mood 1–5, tags, a private note; `wellbeing_alerts`: ASKED or PATTERN, OPEN → SEEN → CLOSED). **Off until the student turns it on** (Help & support → **Wellbeing**, `/student/wellbeing`), after an explanation of what it looks at, what it never does (no AI on personal data; teachers never see it) and who hears about it. **Rules, no AI** (`src/lib/wellbeing.ts`, 5 tests): late nights (handed-in work or AI help between midnight and 5 a.m. in the student's own time zone, ≥ 3 nights in 7), “I'm lost” in in-person classes (D7, ≥ 3 in 14 days), missed deadlines (assignments and quizzes due in the last 14 days and never handed in, ≥ 2), a heavy few days (≥ 4 due in the next 3), hard days (mood ≤ 2 on ≥ 3 of 7, or a very hard day lately) → private nudges with a link (planner, assignments, tutor, Talk to someone); a good week gets a kind word. **Counsellors** are staff given the new **`wellbeing.counsel`** permission (custom roles, B15.6; Office → **Wellbeing requests**, `/admin/wellbeing`; admins reach it from Safety); if nobody has it, requests go to the school admins (the student is told which). They hear only when the student taps **Talk to someone** (one open request at a time, an optional message), or, only if the student ticked the box, when check-ins show a really hard stretch (mood ≤ 2 on ≥ 4 of 7 days, or very hard on 2 of the last 3), at most once a fortnight, never with the notes. **Mark as seen** tells the student someone will reach out. The daily cron sends a private in-app nudge (no email) at most every 3 days to up to 25 students per run, and deletes check-ins older than 90 days. **Turn off and delete** removes the settings and check-ins and withdraws unseen requests. A crisis note is always on the page. APIs: `/api/wellbeing` (GET, POST join / settings / check-in / ask / leave), `/api/wellbeing/requests` (GET, POST seen / close). 6 server tests, 18 API checks, Playwright + axe at 390 px light and dark. **Not done:** a counsellor's reply inside UniVerse (they reach out their usual way); local crisis numbers per school. |

| D17 | Offline classroom relay (experimental) | **Done (moving QR codes; the owner's choice)** | No migration. Research first: phone-to-phone WebRTC over a classroom hotspot isn't reliable enough (QR handshakes per student with no internet, client isolation on many networks, `.local` addresses, no Web Bluetooth sending), so the owner chose **moving QR codes** for short items. New library **jsQR 1.4.0** (Apache-2.0; reads codes on phones without a built-in reader; only in the scanner's own chunk). **`src/lib/qr.ts`**: versions up to 15 (was 10) and QR's compact **alphanumeric mode** when the text allows (byte mode otherwise, so links are unchanged). **`src/lib/qr-relay.ts`** (6 tests, including encoder → jsQR read-back at all three sizes): an item (`NOTE` / `ANNOUNCEMENT` / `QUIZ` with questions and options only) as `[format][flags][key id][Ed25519 signature][deflated JSON]`, Base45 (RFC 9285), cut into frames `UV1:<loop id>:<n>/<total>:<data>` of 180 / 330 / 570 characters (QR versions 7 / 11 / 15); `FrameCollector` takes frames in any order, ignores other codes and a stray frame of another loop, switches when the teacher shows something else; `checkRelay` checks the signature with WebCrypto (`ok` / `bad` / `unsigned` / `unchecked`). **`src/server/qr-relay.ts`**: `GET /api/classroom/relay?courseId=` (the course's announcements and quizzes, each with why it can't be sent: pictures, exam mode, draft, closed, too long, over 40 questions) and `POST` (a note typed now, an announcement or a quiz; built from the database, signed with the platform key `CREDENTIAL_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY` via new `CredentialSigner.signText`; unsigned when there's no key); the course's teacher or an admin only. Sample stub. **Teacher:** Live class → **Send to phones with no internet** (`components/live/QrRelayPanel.tsx`): Note / Announcement / Quiz → **Show as codes**: full screen, the loop at Slow / Normal / Fast (2 / 4 / 6 codes a second), Small / Medium / Large codes, Pause and Next code, "Signed"; prepared items stay **ready on this computer** (up to 8, browser storage) and show with no connection. **Students:** Offline courses → **From the teacher’s screen** → **Scan from the teacher’s screen** (`components/offline/QrRelayScan.tsx`, camera in `QrRelayCamera.tsx`: the phone's BarcodeDetector where there is one, else jsQR): progress per code, then the item is checked against the school's key (kept from `/api/passport/jwks` while online; an item that fails is never saved, one that couldn't be checked yet is checked next time online) and kept on the phone (Cache Storage, 30 items): notes and announcements to read, quizzes to take offline with the existing offline quiz (answers go to the outbox and are marked on the server when online). Opening Offline courses online fetches the scanner ahead so it works offline. Live class (student) links to it (`?scan=1`); search and help entries. **Also fixed:** the offline bar's white text on orange/green (2.2:1) is dark now; the Offline page's own offline note is darker; the phone header's "Home" link is named "UniVerse home" (it shows "UniVerse"). **Tested locally:** API script (24 checks: who may list and prepare, every refusal reason, note / announcement / quiz prepared and signed, no answers in a quiz, the signature against the public key, the whole trip for each kind, a changed item fails, the quiz's answers marked on the server); Playwright end to end (24 checks: the teacher shows a note and a quiz, the codes are read off the screen into a fake camera video; a 390 px student phone **offline** reads them in under 2 s, "Checked: sent by Dr. Jane Smith", keeps it, takes the quiz offline, and back online it's sent and marked 3 of 3; a 5,500-character note in 7 small codes read in 2.5 s); axe clean light and dark (teacher page and code screen, student page, scanner, note, quiz). **Not tried:** real phones and a real projector (the owner's school); iPhone (Safari) reading; the BarcodeDetector path (Chrome on Linux has none). |

| FREE.1 | Live updates reach everyone in big groups (Workers Free) | **Done** | No migration. Telling a person's live hub is one subrequest and Workers Free allows 50 per request, so a message in a 100-member group reached only about the first 50 live. **`src/lib/fanout.ts`** (5 tests, simulating the 50 rule for 100 and 5,000 people): a sender tells at most 40 hubs itself; for more it hands each hub a slice ("relay": that hub tells its own tabs, then the rest of its slice, with its own allowance), so any number of people is reached in a few hops, still one hub request per person. `RealtimeHub` answers `/relay` (`cloudflare/worker.ts`); `deliver()` in `src/server/realtime.ts` uses it. The 40-person caps on class, group, module, call and companion updates are gone; typing indicators are skipped above the plan's live-push size (40 free, 500 paid). **Tested locally (built Worker):** 101 people with UniVerse open (one live connection each, test accounts), a message in their 101-member group reached 101 / 101 in 0.28 s, nobody twice. |
| FREE.2 | Warning before the free plan's daily allowances run out | **Done** | **Migration 0129** (`usage_guard.daily`, `dailyAlerted`). Workers Free has daily allowances (100,000 requests, 100,000 live-hub requests, 13,000 GB-s, 5 M rows read, 100,000 rows written; reset 00:00 UTC = 5:30 AM India) and nothing warned before them. The spending guard (`cloudflare/usage-guard.ts`, every 15 min) now reads today's use too and warns the owners in the app and by push (no email) at 70 % and 90 %, once each a day (`/api/cron/usage-alert`). Also fixed: on the free plan the guard could pause the app from the $5 plan's monthly figures; now only files (R2) can pause it there. Console → Server shows "Cloudflare free plan · today"; the owner's morning summary and insights use the same figures (`src/server/plan-usage.ts`). 4 tests (`src/server/usage-guard.test.ts`). Needs `CF_ACCOUNT_ID` / `CF_USAGE_TOKEN` (DEPLOY-CLOUDFLARE.md); `WORKERS_PAID=true` after upgrading. |
| Q1 | Pages with an id no longer start Next.js (no more "Error 1101" on them) | **Done** | No migration. The 24 client pages (+ `/receipt/[id]`, now a client page reading the new `GET /api/receipts/[id]`, public by id, completed payments only, sample stub) are built once with every parameter "open" (`generateStaticParams` in each `page.tsx`; the old code is `View.tsx`, which reads the values from the address with **`usePathParams`** (`src/hooks/usePathParams.ts`, `src/lib/path-params.ts`; nothing id-specific renders before hydration, so no #418) and shows `AddressLoading` meanwhile). **`scripts/gen-api-routes.mjs`** now also writes **`cloudflare/page-routes.gen.ts`** (every page in Next.js's match order, with the built page for each; `--check` and `src/lib/page-routes.test.ts` keep it current: run it after adding a page). **`cloudflare/page-match.ts`** picks the page like Next.js and the Worker answers `/docs/<id>` with `/docs/open` (from the cache, Next.js not started); `/join/<code>` and `/student/courses/<id>` are 302s from the Worker, keeping the query. **Trap found and fixed:** Next.js's router knows such a page as `/docs/open` while the address says `/docs/<id>`; a link's prefetch to or from one filed the data under one name and looked under the other, and asked again (300 requests in 5 s on the assignments list, which tripped the tab's request brake). The Worker answers those prefetches with 204 (to a built page, or with `Next-Url` naming one); a click loads the page as usual. Also: the whiteboard's menu button and the code editor have accessible names; contrast on the scholarship check page; the receipt page closes its 404 answer. **Tested locally (built Worker, fresh start each time):** all 28 addresses answer in 0.03–0.10 s, and a page that needs Next.js right after still takes 1.8–3.2 s; Playwright at 390 px (light and dark, axe) and 1280 px: every page opens directly with its own data, no page errors; boards list → board and assignments list → assignment → back, in-app; two profiles in a row show their own data; 10 s on each page: no request loops (0–1 requests). **Note for new pages with an id:** copy one of these (page.tsx + View.tsx), then run `node scripts/gen-api-routes.mjs`. |

no password, no code). **Admins:** the second step offers the passkey first and sends no email unless asked ("Email me a code instead"). **Rate limit:** `AUTH_RATE_LIMITER` 10 a minute for sign-in steps (demo sign-in per address; 11th sign-in attempt in a minute → 429; CSP report stored once without the query; recording link for the class, 403 for others, 401 signed out; passkey removed → no sign-in) and Playwright at 390 px with Chromium's virtual authenticator (add a passkey, axe light and dark on Settings and the sign-in page, sign out, sign in with "Passkey", "This device" in the list). 9 unit tests. **Not done:** the admin second-step screen with a passkey was tested through the API only (a real Firebase sign-in is needed for the page); The console tab itself wasn't opened: being the owner needs a real Google sign-in, which a local test can't do. **Fixed while testing:** the AI chart's weekday labels were a day early west of UTC (a date-only string is read as UTC midnight), and the error screen's "Reference" line was too faint for WCAG contrast. |
| Q4 | A4 Performance budget (finish) | **Done** (LCP/INP p75 for real people: read them in owner console → Analytics → Speed after the deploy) | **No migration.** **Three sizes of every uploaded photo:** the browser makes them before uploading (`src/lib/photo-sizes.ts`: longest side 320, 960 and 1920 px, or fewer when the photo is smaller; WebP, else JPEG, PNG kept for transparency; redrawing drops camera data) and sends them in one request, `POST /api/upload/sized` (multipart `w<width>` parts, 2–4 sizes, same picture type, 4 MB together; covered by the Uploads switch, the costly-calls limit and sample mode). Nothing new in the database: the photo's address says which sizes there are (`src/lib/image-sizes.ts`: key `<24 random>-s320-960-1920`, each smaller size is `<random>-<width>`), so old photos and other files are untouched. `uploadChatFile` tries this first for every photo (chat, albums, call chat, status, stickers, avatars, logos, course board, doc pictures), falling back to the single upload; group posts now use it too. `photoSrcSet(url, shownWidth)` adds `srcset`/`sizes` on avatars (`Avatar` and 7 other avatar spots), chat photos, albums, stickers, the media gallery, inline attachments, group post photos, statuses and campus logos. `sizes` for photos without a fixed box is the widest they can be shown (a bubble: `(max-width: 640px) 82vw, 544px`), because it sets the picture's natural size. `deleteFile` removes the smaller sizes with the photo. `authedFetch` no longer labels a form as JSON. **Measured on the built Worker:** a 4000×3000 photo sent in a chat went up in one request as 320/960/1920 px (22/70/140 KB); a phone with a 3× screen loaded the 960 version, a 1× phone the 320 one, a 1280 px laptop (2×) the 1920 one, each shown at the same size as before; an avatar loaded the 22 KB version instead of the full photo; bad forms (one size, mixed types, SVG, repeated widths, 5 MB) were refused. **N+1 review:** `src/server/services/*` had no loops of queries; fixed elsewhere: the peer tutor list (2 queries per tutor → 2 in all, the next free slot with one window-function query), opening a doc (one query per page above it → one recursive query) and a folder in Files (the same). **Peer tutoring fixes found on the way:** "verified by Learning DNA" was given to every class a student was in, without looking at anything; it now comes from the student's real concept mastery (`conceptMasteryOf` in `src/server/learning-dna.ts`, up to 4 classes with concepts), and a profile save keeps "verified" only where that check agrees. The profile request is cleaned (lengths capped, 20 subjects) and the school is the person's own, never the request's; the public tutor list no longer includes email addresses; a bad `page` no longer fails. **Cache-Control:** the public answers that are the same for everyone already had it (branding, manifest, trust, OpenAPI, LTI keys, passport issuer, sign-in lookup, league and impact-room pages); the public passport and impact reports stay `no-store` so hiding them works at once. **Also fixed (security, own commit):** the OneRoster roster reads answered anyone (see the handoff). |
| Q5.1 | Copy a course to a new term | **Done** | **No migration.** Course board (teacher) → **Copy to a new term**: a new code and name, then dates are cleared or moved by whole weeks (same weekday). Copies modules (unlock rules and release dates), items, materials (the same files: deleting a material never deletes its file), quizzes (as drafts) and their questions, assignments and rubrics, gradebook categories, the question bank, Learning DNA concepts (parents first) and tags, and oral checks; never students, work, grades, attendance, announcements or chats; a module's live class is left for the teacher to date again. **How:** `src/server/course-copy.ts`, `POST /api/courses/[id]/copy` (makes the draft course) then `POST …/copy/step` per stage, 90 rows a step, because Workers Free allows 50 queries per request. Each copied row's id comes from the new course's id and the original's (`copiedId`), so steps can point at each other's rows, a repeated step skips what's there (the dialog's **Try again** carries on where it stopped) and nothing is stored between steps. `COLUMNS` per table keeps each insert under D1's 100 values (checked against the schema by `course-copy.test.ts`). **Tested on the built Worker** with a course of 2 modules (6 items of every kind), a 95-question quiz, an assignment with a rubric, a category, bank question, 3 concept tags and an oral check: 14 requests; every item, unlock rule, tag and category points at the copy's own rows; dates moved 52 weeks; a step run twice copied 0; a student is refused (403), copying into itself too (400), a taken code gets 409. |

Students can hide their name from classmates (the teacher always sees it; teachers can't post anonymously). Filters All / Unanswered / Mine and search. **Teacher's AI draft:** "Draft an answer with AI" fills the reply box from the course's own materials with [n] citations (`coursePassages`, one AI request via `spendAi`, on the AI receipt as "Discussion answer drafts", covered by the AI switch and the costly-calls limit); nothing is posted by itself. In-app notices only (new question → teacher; reply → asker; marked as the answer / endorsed → that reply's author), each opening the thread on the person's own board; live refresh for the people in the thread. Personal data export picks the tables up by itself. **Code:** `src/server/course-discussion.ts` (2 tests), `/api/courses/[id]/discussion` (+ `/[threadId]`, `/[threadId]/suggest`), `components/dashboard/CourseDiscussion.tsx`, sample-mode thread. **Tested on the built Worker:** 20 API checks (anonymous for classmates but not the teacher, own-vote refused, vote toggles, accept only by asker/teacher, endorse/lock only by the teacher, locked threads refuse replies, others can't edit, deleting the accepted reply makes it unanswered again, filters and search, the AI draft is teacher-only and says when there are no materials, another course's address 404) and the notices. |

`assertManagesCourse` counts co-teachers, the new `assertGradesCourse` assistants too (attendance); the gradebook, quiz access (`manageableQuiz`, quizzes service), the tutor's teacher view, consent forms, early warning, support plans, marketplace import, office notes, impact verification and parent messages count co-teachers; | Q5.8 | Import and export as Common Cartridge | **Done** (tried on the built Worker: a Canvas-style test cartridge imported in the browser and the course exported and read back; 390 and 1280 px, light and dark, axe clean). Google Classroom: **[owner]**, see the note | **No migration.** Course board → Modules → **Import or export** (teachers and co-teachers). **Export**: the modules as an IMS Common Cartridge 1.1 (`.imscc`, which Canvas, Moodle, Blackboard, Brightspace and Schoology import), made in the browser (`src/lib/cc-export.ts`, zipped with fflate): pages as HTML (rich pages with their blocks written out: a quick check as the question, choices and answer, flashcards as a list, videos as links with their chapters, maths as LaTeX), links and videos as web links, course files inside the cartridge (up to 25 MB each, 300 MB in all; bigger ones as links), quizzes as QTI 1.2 multiple choice with the right answer and points, assignments as pages with their instructions, points and due date. Live classes and students' work stay here. One read for the structure (`GET /api/courses/[id]/cartridge`), then rich pages one by one. **Import**: choose a `.imscc` (or zip, up to 300 MB): it's read in the browser (`src/lib/cc-import.ts` with a small XML reader, `src/lib/xml-lite.ts`: no external entities) and shown first: the cartridge's title, how many pages, links, videos, files and quizzes, its modules, and what won't come in and why (external LTI tools, essay and other non-multiple-choice questions, missing files, links that aren't web addresses). The folders become modules (folders inside folders become their own modules after them), **hidden from students** until the teacher shows them. Pages go into the page editor's format (pictures from the cartridge uploaded, links into the old system removed, scripts dropped, the repeated title at the top removed); files are uploaded and become course materials; YouTube links become videos; discussion topics and assignments come in as pages with their text; QTI multiple-choice and true/false questions become **draft quizzes**. Sent a few items at a time (`POST /api/courses/[id]/import`, at most 20 items, 24 database writes and 250 kB a request: `importBatches`), so a big course stays within Workers Free limits; it can be stopped, and what was added stays. The server checks everything again (pages like the editor's, links, files only if uploaded here, quiz questions with distinct choices and a right answer). Sample mode: export works, import says it isn't saved. 18 unit tests (`cc-export.test.ts`, `cc-import.test.ts`: XML, a Canvas-style cartridge, QTI, paths, pictures, reading back our own export). **Google Classroom** has no cartridge export and its Takeout format isn't documented: importing it needs a sample Takeout export from the owner, or the Classroom API (an app registration, **[owner]**). |

The rules are pure functions (`src/lib/assignment-policy.ts`, 7 tests). Teacher's assignment page → **Rules and extensions**: late penalty (% per started day after the grace minutes, up to a maximum), how many hand-ins before grading (empty: any), saved with the assignment and copied with a course to a new term; extensions (the teaching team: student, new date, extra hand-ins, a note; they're told in the app, and a closed assignment stays open for them until then). A hand-in is measured against the student's own due date (their extension, or the due date plus their extra days); its late days and % are saved then, and taken off when it's graded (the grader shows "→ 6.4 after −20% late" and a box to waive it); a third hand-in over a limit of 2 is refused. **Let them hand in again** on a returned answer (REOPENED: the last grade shows until the new one is graded; it counts as returned in the to-grade counts). Students see their own due date ("your own date"), the rules in a sentence and hand-ins left, and on the grade "8/10 less 20% for handing it in 2 days late" or that it was waived. **Extra time** (Class list → the clock by a student: teacher and co-teachers, audited): extra days on every assignment, and extra % on timed quizzes (a 10-minute quiz becomes 15 with 50%; the exam-mode server clock too). API tried on the Worker: every rule above, and who may do what (students and TAs can't set extra time, students can't give extensions or reopen, students not in the course refused, past dates refused). Sample mode: the new actions say they aren't saved. |

| Fix 16 Oct | The Cloudflare build, CPU limit errors on missing files, a link loop | **Done** | **Build:** the deploy build (main, after PR #61) ran 29 minutes and failed; Cloudflare's build machine has 8 GB, 2 CPUs and 20 minutes. Locally the webpack build was killed for memory. `next.config.ts` now runs webpack in its own process (`webpackBuildWorker`, not on by default with a custom webpack config), with `webpackMemoryOptimizations` and no pre-render source maps, and doesn't type-check again (`pnpm typecheck` runs before every commit); `package.json` gives it 6 GB. Built locally on 2 CPUs: 5 min 40 s, peak memory 6.9 GB for the whole machine (the webpack process about 5.5 GB). **Worth turning on (owner):** Workers & Pages → universe-web → Settings → Build → Build cache, so later builds reuse Next.js's compile cache. **Worker:** a request for a `/_next/static/` file the deployed version doesn't have (an open tab or the installed app asking for an earlier version's scripts) started the whole Next.js server and failed with "Worker exceeded CPU time limit" (88 errors in two minutes on 16 Oct); `cloudflare/worker.ts` now answers 404 at once (7 ms locally); files that exist are served before the Worker as before. **Links:** the teacher's Live class page asked for /boards about 20 times a second (a prefetching `next/link`), which tripped the tab's request brake (`src/lib/request-guard.ts`) and would have used up requests; 11 files now use `@/components/ui/Link` (no prefetching), as the house rule says. |

Booking a time (with a topic) makes a **video call in a one-to-one chat**, so it shows in both people's **Calls** with the usual 15-minute reminder, puts it in **both calendars** (batch `mentor:<booking>`) and tells the mentor; either side can cancel (the call and calendar entries go, the time is offered again). Up to 3 upcoming bookings a student. Two people booking the same time at once: exactly one gets it (the time is claimed with one conditional update; D1 has no transactions). **Be a mentor** (students and alumni on that page, teachers on Volunteer & Mentor): a profile (what you do, where, skills, bio, alumni + year, taking bookings on/off) and times (15–60 min, once or weekly up to 8 weeks, within 60 days, no overlaps, up to 40 ahead). **Job alerts**: a new opening (or one opened later) whose skills are at least half covered by a student's checked skills (`skillMatcher`) notifies them (bell + push, type `job`, in the School group), **once per opening** (`internships.alertedAt`), not if they applied, switch on Skills match (on by default). Openings now take **skills** (the admin form's new "Skills it needs" field; `skills` was silently dropped before, so skills match had nothing to match against for new openings). **Fixed on the way:** the Career page made up an "apply by" date (30 days from today) for openings without a deadline (now none is shown); the mentor list sent every mentor's **email** to anyone signed in (removed); the teacher's Volunteer & Mentor page showed a made-up "45 Verified Partners", a "Request Match" that booked a fake time (today, "10:00 AM") and an email button (now real counts, a Message button that opens a chat, and the teacher's own mentoring). **Migration 0158** (`job_applications`, `interview_answers`, `mentor_slots`; `mentor_profiles.isAlumni` / `gradYear` / `bio`; `mentorship_bookings.slotId` / `scheduledCallId`; `users.jobAlerts`; `internships.alertedAt`). `src/server/careers.ts`, `src/lib/careers.ts`, `src/components/careers/MentorSetup.tsx`; routes `/api/careers/applications`, `/interview`, `/mentors`, `/mentoring`, `/alerts`. AI use kind `interview`. Sample mode answers all five (and the teacher's mentor list), with a sample tracker and mentors. Help: `application-tracker`, `interview-practice`, `mentors`, `mentor-teacher`. 9 unit tests (`src/lib/careers.test.ts`) + 2 help tests; the cron's cheap-check test now expects 12 checks. **Browser test** (19 Oct, `scripts/checks/careers-ui.mjs` on the built Worker, 390 / 1280 px, light / dark): added an application with a link (http made https) and a document, Coming up shows it, stage moved to Interview, the cron sent "Phone screen: … at Test Co" once, edited notes, removed it; interview questions without AI (6), an answer gave 4 fillers (um ×2, like, you know), all four STAR parts and the length note, kept in history, removed; Alice booked the teacher's time from the page: a 30-minute call in their chat, 2 calendar entries, the teacher told and the call in their Calls; cancelled: call and entries gone, time free again; Alice became an alumni mentor (2025) and added a time, Bob sees her; the job alerts switch; the teacher's page has no made-up numbers or email links; sample mode shows the sample tracker, mentors and questions.
